\title{Recursive Formulae for Trotter-Suzuki Decompositions}

\begin{document}

\section*{Introduction}

In this document we gather the results of our derivations within the Mathematica notebook (Efficient\_Trotterizations.nb).
First we define our basis for each order $n$, which is written in terms of right-nested commutators as described by Arnal \textit{et al.} (2021).
We also symmetrize the basis according to the symmetry $A \leftrightarrow B$, which allows us to derive the recursive formulae for one of the steps only (we choose the step with the $A$ operator).
For orders $n \geq 6$ there also exist identities, which help reduce the total number of basis commutators.
We also write down these relations.

In the second part we present the recursive formulae for the scheme coefficients $\alpha_{A}^{(i, i-1)}$, $\beta_{A}^{(i, i-1)}$, $\gamma_{j, A}^{(i, i-1)}$, \ldots
These are written down in terms of the step done with the commutator $A$.
By replacing the parameters $a_{i}$ with $b_{i}$ and the scheme coefficients with their respective counterparts, it is possible to find the formulae for the step in the $B$ operator.
In this formulation the coefficients $\nu$ and $\sigma$ simply add up the scheme parameters as such:

%nu
\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \nu^{(i)} &= \nu^{(i-1)} + a_{i},
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}

% sigma
\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \sigma^{(i-1)} &= \sigma^{(i-2)} + b_{i}.
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}

\vfill

\newpage

\subsection*{Basis}

\subsubsection*{$\mathbf{2}^{\textrm{nd}}$ order}

\vspace{0.1cm}

\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \nonumber
    C_{1} &= \left [ A^{2} B \right ], & \tilde{\alpha} &= - \frac{1}{24}, \\
    C_{2} &= \left [ B^{2} A \right ], & \tilde{\beta} &= \frac{1}{12}.
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}

\vspace{0.1cm}

\subsubsection*{$\mathbf{4}^{\textrm{th}}$ order}

\vspace{0.1cm}

\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \nonumber
    D_{1} &= \left [ A^{4} B \right ] = \left [ A^{2} C_{1} \right ],  & \tilde{\gamma}_{1} &= \frac{7}{5760}, \\
    D_{2} &= \left [ A^{2} B A B \right ] = - \left [ A^{2} C_{2} \right ] = \left [ A B A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ A B C_{1} \right ],  & \tilde{\gamma}_{2} &= \frac{1}{480}, \\
    D_{3} &= \left [ B A^{3} B \right ] = \left [ B A C_{1} \right ],  & \tilde{\gamma}_{3} &= \frac{1}{360}, \\
    D_{4} &= \left [ A B^{3} A \right ] = \left [ A B C_{2} \right ],  & \tilde{\gamma}_{4} &= \frac{1}{360}, \\
    D_{5} &= \left [ B^{2} A B A \right ] = - \left [ B^{2} C_{1} \right ] = \left [ B A B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ B A C_{2} \right ],  & \tilde{\gamma}_{5} &= - \frac{1}{120}, \\
    D_{6} &= \left [ B^{4} A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} C_{2} \right ],  & \tilde{\gamma}_{6} &= -\frac{1}{720}.
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}

\vspace{0.1cm}

\subsubsection*{$\mathbf{6}^{\textrm{th}}$ order}

\vspace{0.1cm}

\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \nonumber
    E_{1} &= \left [ A^{6} B \right ] = \left [ A^{2} D_{1} \right ], & \tilde{\delta}_{1} &= -\frac{31}{967 680}, \\
    E_{2} &= \left [ A^{2} B A^{3} B \right ] = \left [ A^{2} D_{3} \right ], & \tilde{\delta}_{2} &= -\frac{19}{80 640}, \\
    E_{3} &= \left [ A B A^{4} B \right ] = \left [ A B D_{1} \right ], & \tilde{\delta}_{3} &= \frac{23}{161 280}, \\
    E_{4} &= \left [ B A^{5} B \right ] = \left [ B A D_{1} \right ], & \tilde{\delta}_{4} &= -\frac{1}{10 080}, \\
    E_{5} &= \left [ A^{2} B A B A B \right ] = \left [ A^{2} B^{2} A^{2} B \right ] = -\left [ A^{2} D_{5} \right ], & \tilde{\delta}_{5} &= -\frac{1}{10 080}, \\
    E_{6} &= \left [ A B A^{2} B A B \right ] = \left [ A B A B A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ A B D_{2} \right ], & \tilde{\delta}_{6} &= -\frac{1}{20 160}, \\
    E_{7} &= \left [ A B^{2} A^{3} B \right ] = \left [ A B D_{3} \right ], & \tilde{\delta}_{7} &= \frac{13}{60 480}, \\
    E_{8} &= \left [ B A B A^{3} B \right ] = \left [ B A D_{3} \right ], & \tilde{\delta}_{8} &= -\frac{1}{5040}, \\
    E_{9} &= \left [ B A^{3} B A B \right ] = \left [ B A^{2} B A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ B A D_{2} \right ], & \tilde{\delta}_{9} &= -\frac{1}{3360}, \\
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}

\vspace{0.1cm}

\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \nonumber
    E_{10} &= \left [ A B^{3} A B A \right ] = \left [ A B^{2} A B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ A B D_{5} \right ], & \tilde{\delta}_{10} &= -\frac{19}{40 320}, \\
    E_{11} &= \left [ A B A B^{3} A \right ] = \left [ A B D_{4} \right ], & \tilde{\delta}_{11} &= \frac{1}{6720}, \\
    E_{12} &= \left [ B A^{2} B^{3} A \right ] = \left [ B A D_{4} \right ], & \tilde{\delta}_{12} &= -\frac{1}{7560}, \\
    E_{13} &= \left [ B A B^{2} A B A \right ] = \left [ B A B A B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ B A D_{5} \right ], & \tilde{\delta}_{13} &= \frac{1}{1008}, \\
    E_{14} &= \left [ B^{2} A B A B A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} A^{2} B^{2} A \right ] = -\left [ B^{2} D_{2} \right ], & \tilde{\delta}_{14} &= -\frac{1}{10 080}, \\
    E_{15} &= \left [ A B^{5} A \right ] = \left [ A B D_{6} \right ], & \tilde{\delta}_{15} &= -\frac{1}{10 080}, \\
    E_{16} &= \left [ B A B^{4} A \right ] = \left [ B A D_{6} \right ], & \tilde{\delta}_{16} &= \frac{1}{10 080}, \\
    E_{17} &= \left [ B^{2} A B^{3} A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} D_{4} \right ], & \tilde{\delta}_{17} &= \frac{1}{5040}, \\
    E_{18} &= \left [ B^{6} A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} D_{6} \right ], & \tilde{\delta}_{18} &= \frac{1}{30 240}.
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}

\vspace{0.5cm}

\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \nonumber
    \left [ A^{4} B A B \right ] = \left [ A^{3} B A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ A^{2} D_{2} \right ] &= 2 E_{2} - E_{3}, \\
    \left [ A^{3} B^{2} A B \right ] = - \left [ A^{2} D_{4} \right ] &= 3 E_{5} - 3 E_{6} + E_{7}, \\
    \left [ B^{2} A^{4} B \right ] = \left [ B^{2} D_{1} \right ] &= 2 E_{8} - E_{9}, \\
    \left [ A^{2} B^{4} A \right ] = \left [ A^{2} D_{6} \right ] &= 2 E_{11} - E_{10}, \\
    \left [ B^{3} A^{2} B A \right ] = - \left [ B^{2} D_{3} \right ] &= 3 E_{14} - 3 E_{13} + E_{12}, \\
    \left [ B^{4} A B A \right ] = \left [ B^{3} A B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} D_{5} \right ] &= 2 E_{17} - E_{16}.
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}

\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}

    F_{2} &= \left [ A^{3} B A^{4} B \right ] = \left [ A^{2} E_{3} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{2} &= \frac{11}{774 144}, \\

    F_{6} &= \left [ A^{3} B^{2} A^{3} B \right ] = \left [ A^{2} E_{7} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{6} &= \frac{1}{290 304}, \\
    F_{7} &= \left [ A^{2} B A B A^{3} B \right ] = \left [ A^{2} E_{8} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{7} &= - \frac{1}{48 384},
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}

    F_{9} &= \left [ A B A^{2} B A^{3} B \right ] = \left [ A B E_{2} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{9} &= - \frac{1}{13 824}, \\
    F_{10} &= \left [ A B A B A^{4} B \right ] = \left [ A B E_{3} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{10} &= \frac{1}{15 360}, \\
    F_{11} &= \left [ A B^{2} A^{5} B \right ] = \left [ A B E_{4} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{11} &= - \frac{23}{1 612 800}, \\
    F_{12} &= \left [ B A^{3} B A^{3} B \right ] = \left [ B A E_{2} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{12} &= \frac{211}{4 838 400}, \\
    F_{13} &= \left [ B A^{2} B A^{4} B \right ] = \left [ B A E_{3} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{13} &= - \frac{37}{1 209 600}, \\
    F_{14} &= \left [ B A B A^{5} B \right ] = \left [ B A E_{4} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{14} &= \frac{7}{691 200}, \\
    F_{15} &= \left [ A^{2} B A^{2} B^{2} A B \right ] = - \left [ A^{2} E_{12} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{15} &= \frac{47}{1 209 600}, \\
    F_{16} &= \left [ A^{2} B A B^{2} A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ A^{2} B A B A B A B \right ] = - \left [ A^{2} E_{13} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{16} &= - \frac{281}{2 419 200}, \\
    F_{17} &= \left [ A^{2} B^{2} A B A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ A^{2} B^{2} A^{2} B A B \right ] = - \left [ A^{2} E_{14} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{17} &= \frac{163}{1 612 800}, \\
    F_{18} &= \left [ A B A^{2} B A B A B \right ] = \left [ A B A^{2} B^{2} A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ A B E_{5} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{18} &= \frac{73}{1 612 800}, \\
    F_{19} &= \left [ A B A B A^{2} B A B \right ] = \left [ A B A B A B A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ A B E_{6} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{19} &= - \frac{43}{2 419 200}, \\
    F_{20} &= \left [ A B A B^{2} A^{3} B \right ] = \left [ A B E_{7} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{20} &= - \frac{79}{1 209 600}, \\
    F_{21} &= \left [ A B^{2} A B A^{3} B \right ] = \left [ A B E_{8} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{21} &= - \frac{1}{193 536}, \\
    F_{22} &= \left [ A B^{2} A^{3} B A B \right ] = \left [ A B^{2} A^{2} B A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ A B E_{9} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{22} &= - \frac{13}{3 225 600}, \\
    F_{23} &= \left [ B A^{3} B A B A B \right ] = \left [ B A^{3} B^{2} A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ B A E_{5} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{23} &= - \frac{1}{25 200}, \\
    F_{24} &= \left [ B A^{2} B A^{2} B A B \right ] = \left [ B A^{2} B A B A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ B A E_{6} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{24} &= \frac{1}{11 200}, \\
    F_{25} &= \left [ B A^{2} B^{2} A^{3} B \right ] = \left [ B A E_{7} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{25} &= - \frac{1}{75 600}, \\
    F_{26} &= \left [ B A B A B A^{3} B \right ] = \left [ B A E_{8} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{26} &= \frac{1}{12 600}, \\
    F_{27} &= \left [ B A B A^{3} B A B \right ] = \left [ B A B A^{2} B A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ B A E_{9} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{27} &= - \frac{1}{25 200}, \\
    F_{28} &= \left [ B^{2} A B A^{4} B \right ] = \left [ B^{2} E_{3} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{28} &= - \frac{1}{67 200}, \\
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}

\vspace{0.5cm}

\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \nonumber
    F_{29} &= \left [ A^{2} B A B^{4} A \right ] = \left [ A^{2} E_{16} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{29} &= - \frac{1}{67 200}, \\
    F_{30} &= \left [ A B A B^{3} A B A \right ] = \left [ A B A B^{2} A B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ A B E_{10} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{30} &= - \frac{31}{403 200}, \\
    F_{31} &= \left [ A B A B A B^{3} A \right ] = \left [ A B E_{11} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{31} &= \frac{11}{201 600}, \\
    F_{32} &= \left [ A B^{2} A^{2} B^{3} A \right ] = \left [ A B E_{12} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{32} &= - \frac{1}{1 209 600}, \\
    F_{33} &= \left [ A B^{2} A B^{2} A B A \right ] = \left [ A B^{2} A B A B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ A B E_{13} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{33} &= \frac{17}{134 400}, \\
    F_{34} &= \left [ A B^{3} A B A B A \right ] = \left [ A B^{3} A^{2} B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ A B E_{14} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{34} &= - \frac{1}{19 200}, \\
    F_{35} &= \left [ B A^{2} B^{3} A B A \right ] = \left [ B A^{2} B^{2} A B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ B A E_{10} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{35} &= \frac{31}{604 800}, \\
    F_{36} &= \left [ B A^{2} B A B^{3} A \right ] = \left [ B A E_{11} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{36} &= - \frac{1}{60 480}, \\
    F_{37} &= \left [ B A B A^{2} B^{3} A \right ] = \left [ B A E_{12} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{37} &= - \frac{1}{50 400}, \\
    F_{38} &= \left [ B A B A B^{2} A B A \right ] = \left [ B A B A B A B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ B A E_{13} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{38} &= - \frac{1}{5400}, \\
    F_{39} &= \left [ B A B^{2} A B A B A \right ] = \left [ B A B^{2} A^{2} B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ B A E_{14} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{39} &= \frac{1}{12 600}, \\
    F_{40} &= \left [ B^{2} A^{2} B A B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} A^{2} B^{2} A B A \right ] = - \left [ B^{2} E_{5} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{40} &= \frac{1}{12 600}, \\
    F_{41} &= \left [ B^{2} A B A^{2} B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} A B A B A B A \right ] = - \left [ B^{2} E_{6} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{41} &= - \frac{11}{151 200}, \\
    F_{42} &= \left [ B^{2} A B^{2} A^{2} B A \right ] = - \left [ B^{2} E_{7} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{42} &= \frac{1}{100 800}, \\
    F_{43} &= \left [ A B A B^{5} A \right ] = \left [ A B E_{15} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{43} &= - \frac{29}{1 209 600}, \\
    F_{44} &= \left [ A B^{2} A B^{4} A \right ] = \left [ A B E_{16} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{44} &= \frac{83}{1 209 600}, \\
    F_{45} &= \left [ A B^{3} A B^{3} A \right ] = \left [ A B E_{17} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{45} &= - \frac{31}{1 209 600}, \\
    F_{46} &= \left [ B A^{2} B^{5} A \right ] = \left [ B A E_{15} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{46} &= \frac{1}{50 400}, \\
    F_{47} &= \left [ B A B A B^{4} A \right ] = \left [ B A E_{16} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{47} &= - \frac{1}{10 080}, \\
    F_{48} &= \left [ B A B^{2} A B^{3} A \right ] = \left [ B A E_{17} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{48} &= \frac{1}{30 240}, \\
    F_{49} &= \left [ B^{2} A B^{3} A B A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} A B^{2} A B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} E_{10} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{49} &= - \frac{1}{20 160},
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}

\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \nonumber
    F_{50} &= \left [ B^{2} A B A B^{3} A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} E_{11} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{50} &= \frac{1}{15 120}, \\
    F_{51} &= \left [ B^{3} A^{2} B^{3} A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} E_{12} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{51} &= - \frac{1}{90 720}, \\
    F_{52} &= \left [ A B^{7} A \right ] = \left [ A B E_{18} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{52} &= \frac{1}{302 400}, \\
    F_{53} &= \left [ B A B^{6} A \right ] = \left [ B A E_{18} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{53} &= - \frac{1}{75 600}, \\
    F_{54} &= \left [ B^{2} A B^{5} A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} E_{15} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{54} &= \frac{1}{50 400}, \\
    F_{55} &= \left [ B^{3} A B^{4} A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} E_{16} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{55} &= - \frac{1}{60 480}, \\
    F_{56} &= \left [ B^{8} A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} E_{18} \right ], & \tilde{\epsilon}_{56} &= - \frac{1}{1 209 600}.
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}


\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \nonumber
    \left [ A^{4} B A^{3} B \right ] = \left [ A^{2} E_{2} \right ] &= 3 F_{2} - 3 F_{3} + F_{4}, \\
    \left [ A^{6} B A B \right ] = \left [ A^{5} B A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ A^{4} D_{2} \right ] &= 5 F_{2} - 6 F_{3} + 2 F_{4}, \\
    \left [ A^{4} B A B A B \right ] = \left [ A^{4} B^{2} A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ A^{2} E_{5} \right ] &= 4 F_{6} - 14 F_{7} + 9 F_{8} - 16 F_{9} + 22 F_{10} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} - 4 F_{11} + 9 F_{12} - 12 F_{13} + 3 F_{14}, \\
    \left [ A^{3} B A^{2} B A B \right ] = \left [ A^{3} B A B A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ A^{2} E_{6} \right ] &= F_{6} - 4 F_{7} + 4 F_{8} - 6 F_{9} + 7 F_{10} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} - F_{11} + 3 F_{12} - 4 F_{13} + F_{14}, \\
    \left [ B^{2} A^{6} B \right ] = \left [ B^{2} E_{1} \right ] &= F_{12} - 3 F_{13} + 3 F_{14}, \\
    \left [ A^{5} B^{2} A B \right ] = - \left [ A^{4} D_{4} \right ] &= 10 F_{6} - 30 F_{7} + 15 F_{8} - 30 F_{9} + 45 F_{10} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} - 9 F_{11} + 18 F_{12} - 24 F_{13} + 6 F_{14}, \\
    \left [ A^{2} B^{2} A^{4} B \right ] = \left [ A^{2} B^{2} D_{1} \right ] &= 2 F_{7} - F_{8}, \\
    \left [ A B A^{3} B A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ A B A^{4} B A B \right ] = \left [ A B A^{2} D_{2} \right ] &= 2 F_{9} - F_{10}, \\
    \left [ B A^{5} B A B \right ] = \left [ B A^{4} B A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ B A^{3} D_{2} \right ] &= 2 F_{12} - F_{13}, \\
    \left [ A^{3} B^{3} A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ A^{3} B^{2} A B A B \right ] = - \left [ A^{2} E_{10} \right ] &= 2 F_{16} - 2 F_{19} + F_{22}, \\
    \left [ A^{3} B A B^{2} A B \right ] = - \left [ A^{2} E_{11} \right ] &= 4 F_{15} - 5 F_{16} + 3 F_{17} - 3 F_{18} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} + 5 F_{19} - 4 F_{20} + F_{21}, \\
    \left [ B^{2} A^{2} B A^{3} B \right ] = \left [ B^{2} E_{2} \right ] &= - F_{15} + 4 F_{16} - 3 F_{17} - 3 F_{18} + 2 F_{19} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} + 2 F_{20} - F_{22} + 2 F_{23} - 3 F_{24} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} - 2 F_{26} + 3 F_{27} + F_{28}, \\
    \left [ B^{3} A^{5} B \right ] = \left [ B^{2} E_{4} \right ] &= - 2 F_{15} + 8 F_{16} - 6 F_{17} - 6 F_{18} + 4 F_{19} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} + 4 F_{20} - 2 F_{22} + 3 F_{23} - 3 F_{24} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} + F_{25} - 6 F_{26} + 3 F_{27} + 3 F_{28},
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}


\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \nonumber
    \left [ A^{4} B^{3} A B \right ] = - \left [ A^{4} D_{6} \right ] &= 8 F_{15} - 12 F_{16} + 6 F_{17} - 6 F_{18} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} + 12 F_{19} - 8 F_{20} + 2 F_{21} - F_{22}, \\
    \left [ A^{2} B^{3} A^{3} B \right ] = \left [ A^{2} B^{2} D_{3} \right ] &= F_{15} - 3 F_{16} + 3 F_{17}, \\
    \left [ A B A^{3} B^{2} A B \right ] = - \left [ A B A^{2} D_{4} \right ] &= 3 F_{18} - 3 F_{19} + F_{20}, \\
    \left [ A B^{3} A^{4} B \right ] = \left [ A B^{3} D_{1} \right ] &= 2 F_{21} - F_{22}, \\
    \left [ B A^{4} B^{2} A B \right ] = - \left [ B A^{3} D_{4} \right ] &= 3 F_{23} - 3 F_{24} + F_{25}, \\
    \left [ B A B^{2} A^{4} B \right ] = \left [ B A B^{2} D_{1} \right ] &= 2 F_{26} - F_{27}, \\
    \left [ B^{2} A^{4} B A B \right ] = \left [ B^{2} A^{3} B A^{2} B \right ] = \left [ B^{2} A^{2} D_{2} \right ] &= - 2 F_{15} + 8 F_{16} - 6 F_{17} - 6 F_{18} + 4 F_{19} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} + 4 F_{20} - 2 F_{22} + 4 F_{23} - 6 F_{24} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} - 4 F_{26} + 6 F_{27} + F_{28}, \\
    \left [ A^{2} B^{4} A B A \right ] = \left [ A^{2} B^{3} A B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ A^{2} B^{2} D_{5} \right ] &= - 2 F_{42} + 8 F_{41} - 6 F_{40} - 6 F_{39} + 4 F_{38} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} + 4 F_{37} - 2 F_{35} + 4 F_{34} - 6 F_{33} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} - 4 F_{31} + 6 F_{30} + F_{29}, \\
    \left [ A B A^{2} B^{4} A \right ] = \left [ A B A^{2} D_{6} \right ] &= 2 F_{31} - F_{30}, \\
    \left [ A B^{4} A^{2} B A \right ] = - \left [ A B^{3} D_{3} \right ] &= 3 F_{34} - 3 F_{33} + F_{32}, \\
    \left [ B A^{3} B^{4} A \right ] = \left [ B A^{3} D_{6} \right ] &= 2 F_{36} - F_{35}, \\
    \left [ B A B^{3} A^{2} B A \right ] = - \left [ B A B^{2} D_{3} \right ] &= 3 F_{39} - 3 F_{38} + F_{37}, \\
    \left [ B^{2} A^{3} B^{3} A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} A^{2} D_{4} \right ] &= F_{42} - 3 F_{41} + 3 F_{40}, \\
    \left [ B^{4} A^{3} B A \right ] = - \left [ B^{4} D_{1} \right ] &= 8 F_{42} - 12 F_{41} + 6 F_{40} - 6 F_{39} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} + 12 F_{38} - 8 F_{37} + 2 F_{36} - F_{35}, \\
    \left [ A^{3} B^{5} A \right ] = \left [ A^{2} E_{15} \right ] &= - 2 F_{42} + 8 F_{41} - 6 F_{40} - 6 F_{39} + 4 F_{38} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} + 4 F_{37} - 2 F_{35} + 3 F_{34} - 3 F_{33} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} + F_{32} - 6 F_{31} + 3 F_{30} + 3 F_{29}, \\
    \left [ A^{2} B^{2} A B^{3} A \right ] = \left [ A^{2} E_{17} \right ] &= - F_{42} + 4 F_{41} - 3 F_{40} - 3 F_{39} + 2 F_{38} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} + 2 F_{37} - F_{35} + 2 F_{34} - 3 F_{33} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} - 2 F_{31} + 3 F_{30} + F_{29}, \\
    \left [ B^{3} A B A^{2} B A \right ] = - \left [ B^{2} E_{8} \right ] &= 4 F_{42} - 5 F_{41} + 3 F_{40} - 3 F_{39} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} + 5 F_{38} - 4 F_{37} + F_{36}, \\
    \left [ B^{3} A^{3} B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ B^{3} A^{2} B A B A \right ] = - \left [ B^{2} E_{9} \right ] &= 2 F_{41} - 2 F_{38} + F_{35}, \\
    \left [ A B^{5} A B A \right ] = \left [ A B^{4} A B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ A B^{3} D_{5} \right ] &= 2 F_{45} - F_{44}, \\
    \left [ B A B^{3} A B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ B A B^{4} A B A \right ] = \left [ B A B^{2} D_{5} \right ] &= 2 F_{48} - F_{47}, \\
    \left [ B^{2} A^{2} B^{4} A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} A^{2} D_{6} \right ] &= 2 F_{50} - F_{49},
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}

\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \nonumber
    \left [ B^{5} A^{2} B A \right ] = - \left [ B^{4} D_{3} \right ] &= 10 F_{51} - 30 F_{50} + 15 F_{49} - 30 F_{48} + 45 F_{47} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} - 9 F_{46} + 18 F_{45} - 24 F_{44} + 6 F_{43}, \\
    \left [ A^{2} B^{6} A \right ] = \left [ A^{2} E_{18} \right ] &= F_{45} - 3 F_{44} + 3 F_{43}, \\
    \left [ B^{3} A B^{2} A B A \right ] = \left [ B^{3} A B A B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} E_{13} \right ] &= F_{51} - 4 F_{50} + 4 F_{49} - 6 F_{48} + 7 F_{47} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} - F_{46} + 3 F_{45} - 4 F_{44} + F_{43}, \\
    \left [ B^{4} A B A B A \right ] = \left [ B^{4} A^{2} B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} E_{14} \right ] &= 4 F_{51} - 14 F_{50} + 9 F_{49} - 16 F_{48} + 22 F_{47} \\
    &\hspace{0.5cm} - 4 F_{46} + 9 F_{45} - 12 F_{44} + 3 F_{43}, \\
    \left [ B^{6} A B A \right ] = \left [ B^{5} A B^{2} A \right ] = \left [ B^{4} D_{5} \right ] &= 5 F_{55} - 6 F_{54} + 2 F_{53}, \\
    \left [ B^{4} A B^{3} A \right ] = \left [ B^{2} E_{17} \right ] &= 3 F_{55} - 3 F_{54} + F_{53}.
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}

\subsection*{Coefficient recursive formulae}

\subsubsection*{$\mathbf{2}^{\textrm{nd}}$ order}

\vspace{0.2cm}

% alpha
\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \alpha_{A}^{(i, i-1)} &= \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\alpha} a_{i}^{2} \sigma^{(i-1)} - \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \nu^{(i-1)} \sigma^{(i-1)},
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}

% beta
\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \beta_{A}^{(i, i-1)} &= \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2}.
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}

\subsubsection*{$\mathbf{4}^{\textrm{th}}$ order}

\vspace{0.2cm}

% gamma 1
{\small
\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \gamma_{1, A}^{(i, i-1)} &= \gamma_{1, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\gamma}_{1} a_{i}^{4} \sigma^{(i-1)} + \left(\tilde{\gamma}_{2} + \tilde{\gamma}_{3} \right) a_{i}^{3} \nu^{(i-1)} \sigma^{(i-1)} - \left(\tilde{\gamma}_{4} + \tilde{\gamma}_{5} \right) a_{i}^{2} \left(\nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \sigma^{(i-1)} \\
    & - \tilde{\gamma}_{6} a_{i} \left(\nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} \sigma^{(i-1)} + \tilde{\alpha} a_{i}^{2} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \nu^{(i-1)} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)},
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}
}

% gamma 2
{\small
\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \gamma_{2, A}^{(i, i-1)} &= \gamma_{2, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\gamma}_{2} a_{i}^{3} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} - \left(2 \tilde{\gamma}_{4} + \tilde{\gamma}_{5} \right) a_{i}^{2} \nu^{(i-1)} \left(\sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} - 2 \tilde{\gamma}_{6} a_{i} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \\
    & - \tilde{\alpha} a_{i}^{2} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \sigma^{(i-1)} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \nu^{(i-1)} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)},
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}
}

% gamma 3
{\small
\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \gamma_{3, A}^{(i, i-1)} &= \gamma_{3, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\gamma}_{3} a_{i}^{3} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} - \tilde{\gamma}_{5} a_{i}^{2} \nu^{(i-1)} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} - \tilde{\gamma}_{6} a_{i} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \\
    & - 2 \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \sigma^{(i-1)} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)},
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}
}

% gamma 4
{\small
\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \gamma_{4, A}^{(i, i-1)} &= \gamma_{4, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\gamma}_{4} a_{i}^{2} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} + \tilde{\gamma}_{6} a_{i} \nu^{(i-1)} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} + \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \sigma^{(i-1)} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)},
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}
}

% gamma 5
{\small
\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \gamma_{5, A}^{(i, i-1)} &= \gamma_{5, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\gamma}_{5} a_{i}^{2} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} + 2 \tilde{\gamma}_{6} a_{i} \nu^{(i-1)} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} - 2 \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \sigma^{(i-1)} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)},
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}
}

% gamma 6
{\small
\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \gamma_{6, A}^{(i, i-1)} &= \gamma_{6, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\gamma}_{6} a_{i} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{4}.
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}
}

\subsubsection*{$\mathbf{6}^{\textrm{th}}$ order}

\vspace{0.2cm}

% delta 1
{\footnotesize
\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \delta_{1, A}^{(i, i-1)} &= \delta_{1, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\delta}_{1} a_{i}^{6} \sigma^{(i-1)} + \left( \tilde{\delta}_{2} + \tilde{\delta}_{3} + \tilde{\delta}_{4} \right) a_{i}^{5} \nu^{(i-1)} \sigma^{(i-1)} \\
    & + \left( \tilde{\delta}_{5} + \tilde{\delta}_{6} + \tilde{\delta}_{7} + \tilde{\delta}_{8} + \tilde{\delta}_{9} \right) a_{i}^{4} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \sigma^{(i-1)} \\
    & - \left( \tilde{\delta}_{10} + \tilde{\delta}_{11} + \tilde{\delta}_{12} + \tilde{\delta}_{13} + \tilde{\delta}_{14} \right) a_{i}^{3} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} \sigma^{(i-1)} - \left( \tilde{\delta}_{15} + \tilde{\delta}_{16} + \tilde{\delta}_{17} \right) a_{i}^{2} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{4} \sigma^{(i-1)} \\
    & - \tilde{\delta}_{18} a_{i} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{5} \sigma^{(i-1)} + \tilde{\gamma}_{1} a_{i}^4 \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \left( \tilde{\gamma}_{2} + \tilde{\gamma}_{3} \right)  a_{i}^3 \nu^{(i-1)} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \\
    & - \left( \tilde{\gamma}_{4} + \tilde{\gamma}_{5} \right) a_{i}^{2} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - \tilde{\gamma}_{6} a_{i} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\alpha} a_{i}^{2} \gamma_{1, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \nu^{(i-1)} \gamma_{1, B}^{(i-1, i-1)},
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}
}

% delta 2
{\footnotesize
\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \delta_{2, A}^{(i, i-1)} &= \delta_{2, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\delta}_{2} a_{i}^{5} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} + \left( 3 \tilde{\delta}_{5} + 2 \tilde{\delta}_{6} + \tilde{\delta}_{7} + \tilde{\delta}_{8} + 2 \tilde{\delta}_{9} \right) a_{i}^{4} \nu^{(i-1)} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \\
    & - \left( 3 \tilde{\delta}_{10} + 4 \tilde{\delta}_{11} + 4 \tilde{\delta}_{12} + 3 \tilde{\delta}_{13} + 2 \tilde{\delta}_{14} \right) a_{i}^{3} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \\
    & - \left( 5 \tilde{\delta}_{15} + 5 \tilde{\delta}_{16} + 4 \tilde{\delta}_{17} \right) a_{i}^{2} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} - 5 \tilde{\delta}_{18} a_{i} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{4} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} - 2 \tilde{\gamma}_{1} a_{i}^4 \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \\
    & + \tilde{\gamma}_{3} a_{i}^3 \sigma^{(i-1)} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - 2 \left( \tilde{\gamma}_{2} + \tilde{\gamma}_{3} \right) a_{i}^3 \nu^{(i-1)} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - \tilde{\gamma}_{5} a_{i}^{2} \nu^{(i-1)} \sigma^{(i-1)} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \\
    & + 2 \left( \tilde{\gamma}_{4} + \tilde{\gamma}_{5} \right) a_{i}^{2} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - \tilde{\gamma}_{6} a_{i} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \sigma^{(i-1)} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + 2 \tilde{\gamma}_{6} a_{i} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \\
    & + 2 \tilde{\alpha} a_{i}^{2} \gamma_{2, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\alpha} a_{i}^{2} \gamma_{3, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - 2 \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \nu^{(i-1)} \gamma_{2, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \nu^{(i-1)} \gamma_{3, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \left(\alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \right)^{2},
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}
}

% delta 3
{\footnotesize
\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \delta_{3, A}^{(i, i-1)} &= \delta_{3, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\delta}_{3} a_{i}^{5} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} - \left( \tilde{\delta}_{5} - \tilde{\delta}_{7} + \tilde{\delta}_{9} \right) a_{i}^{4} \nu^{(i-1)} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \\
    & + \left( \tilde{\delta}_{11} + 2 \tilde{\delta}_{12} + \tilde{\delta}_{13} \right) a_{i}^{3} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} + \left( \tilde{\delta}_{15} + 2 \tilde{\delta}_{16} + \tilde{\delta}_{17} \right) a_{i}^{2} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \\
    & + \tilde{\delta}_{18} a_{i} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{4} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} + \tilde{\gamma}_{1} a_{i}^4 \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \left(\tilde{\gamma}_{2} - 2 \tilde{\gamma}_{3} \right) a_{i}^3 \sigma^{(i-1)} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \\
    & + \left( \tilde{\gamma}_{2} + \tilde{\gamma}_{3} \right) a_{i}^3 \nu^{(i-1)} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - \left(2 \tilde{\gamma}_{4} - \tilde{\gamma}_{5} \right) a_{i}^{2} \nu^{(i-1)} \sigma^{(i-1)} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - \left( \tilde{\gamma}_{4} + \tilde{\gamma}_{5} \right) a_{i}^{2} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \\
    & - \tilde{\gamma}_{6} a_{i} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - \tilde{\alpha} a_{i}^{2} \gamma_{2, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \sigma^{(i-1)} \gamma_{1, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \\
    & + \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \nu^{(i-1)} \gamma_{2, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + 2 \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \left(\alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \right)^{2},
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}
}

% delta 4
{\footnotesize
\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \delta_{4, A}^{(i, i-1)} &= \delta_{4, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\delta}_{4} a_{i}^{5} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} + \left( \tilde{\delta}_{8} + \tilde{\delta}_{9} \right) a_{i}^{4} \nu^{(i-1)} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \\
    & - \left( \tilde{\delta}_{12} + \tilde{\delta}_{13} + \tilde{\delta}_{14} \right) a_{i}^{3} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} - \left( \tilde{\delta}_{16} + \tilde{\delta}_{17} \right) a_{i}^{2} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \\
    & - \tilde{\delta}_{18} a_{i} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{4} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} + 2 \tilde{\gamma}_{3} a_{i}^3 \sigma^{(i-1)} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - 2 \tilde{\gamma}_{5} a_{i}^{2} \nu^{(i-1)} \sigma^{(i-1)} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \\
    & - 2 \tilde{\gamma}_{6} a_{i} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \sigma^{(i-1)} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - 2 \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \sigma^{(i-1)} \gamma_{1, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \left(\alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \right)^{2},
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}
}

% delta 5
{\footnotesize
\begin{equation}

    \delta_{5, A}^{(i, i-1)} &= \delta_{5, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\delta}_{5} a_{i}^{4} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} - \left( \tilde{\delta}_{10} + 3 \tilde{\delta}_{11} + 3 \tilde{\delta}_{12} + \tilde{\delta}_{13} \right) a_{i}^{3} \nu^{(i-1)}\left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} \\
    & - \left( 5 \tilde{\delta}_{15} + 5 \tilde{\delta}_{16} + 3 \tilde{\delta}_{17} \right) a_{i}^{2} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} - 5 \tilde{\delta}_{18} a_{i} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} + \tilde{\gamma}_{2} a_{i}^{3} \sigma^{(i-1)} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \\
    & - \left( \tilde{\gamma}_{4} + \tilde{\gamma}_{5} \right) a_{i}^{2} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - \left( 2 \tilde{\gamma}_{4} + \tilde{\gamma}_{5} \right) a_{i}^{2} \nu^{(i-1)} \sigma^{(i-1)} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \\
    & - 3 \tilde{\gamma}_{6} a_{i} \nu^{(i-1)} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - 2 \tilde{\gamma}_{6} a_{i} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \sigma^{(i-1)} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - 3 \tilde{\alpha} a_{i}^{2} \gamma_{4, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - \tilde{\alpha} a_{i}^{2} \gamma_{5, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \\
    & + 3 \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \nu^{(i-1)} \gamma_{4, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \nu^{(i-1)} \gamma_{5, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)},
  \end{aligned}

% delta 6
{\footnotesize

    & + \left( \tilde{\delta}_{15} + 3 \tilde{\delta}_{16} + \tilde{\delta}_{17} \right) a_{i}^{2} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} + \tilde{\delta}_{18} a_{i} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} - 3 \tilde{\gamma}_{2} a_{i}^{3} \sigma^{(i-1)} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \\
    & + \left( 3 \tilde{\gamma}_{4} + 2 \tilde{\gamma}_{5} \right) a_{i}^{2} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \left( 6 \tilde{\gamma}_{4} + 3 \tilde{\gamma}_{5} \right) a_{i}^{2} \nu^{(i-1)} \sigma^{(i-1)} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \\
    & + 7 \tilde{\gamma}_{6} a_{i} \nu^{(i-1)} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + 6 \tilde{\gamma}_{6} a_{i} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \sigma^{(i-1)} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + 3 \tilde{\alpha} a_{i}^{2} \gamma_{4, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \\
    & + \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \sigma^{(i-1)} \gamma_{2, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - 3 \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \nu^{(i-1)} \gamma_{4, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - 2 \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)},
  \end{aligned}

}

% delta 7
{\footnotesize
\begin{equation}
  \begin{aligned}
    \delta_{7, A}^{(i, i-1)} &= \delta_{7, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\delta}_{7} a_{i}^{4} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} - \left( \tilde{\delta}_{10} + \tilde{\delta}_{11} + \tilde{\delta}_{12} \right) a_{i}^{3} \nu^{(i-1)}\left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} \\
    & - \left( 2 \tilde{\delta}_{15} + \tilde{\delta}_{16} + \tilde{\delta}_{17} \right) a_{i}^{2} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} - 2 \tilde{\delta}_{18} a_{i} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} \\

    & - \left( \tilde{\epsilon}_{43} + \tilde{\epsilon}_{44} + \tilde{\epsilon}_{45} + \tilde{\epsilon}_{46} + \tilde{\epsilon}_{47} + \tilde{\epsilon}_{48} + \tilde{\epsilon}_{49} + \tilde{\epsilon}_{50} + \tilde{\epsilon}_{51} \right) a_{i}^{3} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{5} \sigma^{(i-1)} - \left( \tilde{\epsilon}_{52} + \tilde{\epsilon}_{53} + \tilde{\epsilon}_{54} + \tilde{\epsilon}_{55} \right) a_{i}^{2} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{6} \sigma^{(i-1)} \\
    & - \tilde{\epsilon}_{56} a_{i} \left( \nu^{(i-1)} \right)^{7} \sigma^{(i-1)} + \tilde{\delta}_{1} a_{i}^{6} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \left( \tilde{\delta}_{2} + \tilde{\delta}_{3} + \tilde{\delta}_{4} \right) a_{i}^{5} \nu^{(i-1)} \alpha_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \\

    & - 4 \tilde{\gamma}_{6} a_{i} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} \gamma_{5, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} - 3 \tilde{\gamma}_{6} a_{i} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{2} \left( \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \right)^{2},
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}
}

% epsilon 52

    \epsilon_{52, A}^{(i, i-1)} &= \epsilon_{52, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\epsilon}_{52} a_{i}^{2} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{7} + \tilde{\epsilon}_{56} a_{i} \nu^{(i-1)} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{7} + \tilde{\delta}_{18} a_{i} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{5} \beta_{B}^{(i-1, i-1)} \\
    & + \tilde{\gamma}_{6} a_{i} \left( \sigma^{(i-1)} \right)^{3} \gamma_{6, B}^{(i-1, i-1)} + \tilde{\beta} a_{i} \sigma^{(i-1)} \delta_{18, B}^{(i-1, i-1)},
  \end{aligned}
\end{equation}
}
